<section class="designed">
<div class="container">
<h2>Unified Data Analytics Platform</h2>
<p class="designed-description">One cloud platform for massive scale data engineering and collaborative data science</p>
<p class="my-2"><a class="btn-try" href="/product/unified-data-analytics-platform">LEARN MORE</a></p>
<div class="row">
<div class="col-md-10 col-md-offset-1 threeColText">
<div class="col-sm-4 my-2">
<p>Increases the reliability, quality, <br class="hidden-xs hidden-sm hidden-md"/>and performance of data lakes</p>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-4 my-2">
<p>Enables collaborative data science <br class="hidden-xs hidden-sm hidden-md"/>and machine learning on all your data</p>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-4 my-2">
<p>Delivers actionable insights and <br class="hidden-xs hidden-sm hidden-md"/>predictions to the business</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="clear"></div>
</div>
</section>
